   appointment = appt_service.get_or_404(db, appointment_id)\r\n    if user is not None:\r\n        appt_service.ensure_visible(db, user, appointment)\r\n    if str(appointment.status) in | None = None,\r\n    rating_default: float = DEFAULT_RATING,\r\n    required_skills: Sequence[str] | None = None,\r\n    top_n: int | None = None,\r\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\r\n    \"\"\"筛选并打分候选护理员，返回按 score 倒序的明细列表。\r\n\r\n    每个候选 dict 支持的键：\r\n        staff_id, name, distance_km, today_count, skills, rating,\r\n        is_focus, service_radius_km, status（可选，非 active 直接剔除）\r\n\r\n    过滤规则（不满足直接剔除，便于\"缺技能/超半径/已达上限被过滤\"的断言）：\r\n        1. status 若提供且不是 active；\r\n        2. 必需技能未全部覆盖；\r\n        3. 距离超出服务半径（取调用方 radius_km 与本人 service_radius_km 的较小值）；\r\n        4. 当日已派工单数 >= 每日上限。\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    parsed_weights = parse_weights(weights)\r\n    required = [str(s) for s in (required_skills or []) if str(s).strip()]\r\n    default_limit = DEFAULT_DAILY_LIMIT if daily_limit is None else int(daily_limit)\r\n    default_radius = DEFAULT_RADIUS_KM if radius_km is None else float(radius_km)\r\n\r\n    scored: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\r\n    for candidate in candidates:\r\n        status = candidate.get(\"status\")\r\n        if status is not None and str(status) != \"active\":\r\n            continue\r\n\r\n        skills = candidate.get(\"skills\") or []\r\n        skill_value = skill_factor(required, skills)\r\n        if required and skill_value < 1.0 - 1e-9:\r\n            continue\r\n\r\n        raw_distance = candidate.get(\"distance_km\")\r\n        distance = math.inf if raw_distance is None else float(raw_distance)\r\n        staff_radius = candidate.get(\"service_radius_km\")\r\n        allowed_radius = default_radius\r\n        if staff_radius is not None:\r\n            allowed_radius = min(allowed_radius, float(staff_radius))\r\n        if not math.isfinite(distance) or distance > allowed_radius:\r\n            continue\r\n\r\n        today_count = int(candidate.get(\"today_count\") or 0)\r\n        limit = default_limit\r\n        if candidate.get(\"daily_limit\") is not None:\r\n            limit = int(candidate[\"daily_limit\"])\r\n        if limit <= 0 or today_count >= limit:\r\n            continue\r\n\r\n        rating = candidate.get(\"rating\")\r\n        factors = {\r\n            \"skill\": round(skill_value, SCORE_DIGITS),\r\n            \"distance\": round(distance_factor(distance, allowed_radius), SCORE_DIGITS),\r\n            \"load\": round(load_factor(today_count, limit), SCORE_DIGITS),\r\n            \"rating\": round(rating_factor(rating, rating_default), SCORE_DIGITS),\r\n        }\r\n        score = weighted_score(factors, parsed_weights)\r\n        is_focus = bool(candidate.get(\"is_focus\"))\r\n        if is_focus:\r\n            score *= FOCUS_PENALTY\r\n\r\n        scored.append(\r\n            {\r\n                \"staff_id\": candidate.get(\"staff_id\"),\r\n                \"name\": candidate.get(\"name\") or \"\",\r\n                \"distance_km\": round(distance, SCORE_DIGITS),\r\n                \"score\": round(score, SCORE_DIGITS),\r\n                \"factors\": factors,\r\n                \"contributions\": {\r\n                    name: round(factors[name] * parsed_weights[index], SCORE_DIGITS)\r\n                    for index, name in enumerate(FACTOR_NAMES)\r\n                },\r\n                \"penalty\": FOCUS_PENALTY if is_focus else 1.0,\r\n                \"reason\": _build_reason(\r\n                    required=required,\r\n                    distance_km=distance,\r\n                    today_count=today_count,\r\n                    rating=rating,\r\n                    default_rating=rating_default,\r\n                    is_focus=is_focus,\r\n                ),\r\n                \"today_count\": today_count,\r\n                \"rating\": None if rating is None else round(float(rating), SCORE_DIGITS),\r\n                \"is_focus\": is_focus,\r\n            }\r\n        )\r\n\r\n    scored.sort(key=lambda row: (-row[\"score\"], row[\"distance_km\"], row[\"staff_id\"] or 0))\r\n    if top_n is not None and top_n >= 0:\r\n        return scored[: int(top_n)]\r\n    return scored\r\n"}}

\"load_score\", \"loadScore\", \"负荷\"]),\r\n                    rating: pickNumber(factors, [\"rating\", \"rating_score\", \"ratingScore\", \"评分\"])\r\n                };\r\n                FACTOR_KEYS.forEach(function (key) {\r\n                    if (flat[key] === null) {\r\n                        var alt = pickNumber(c, [key, key + \"_score\", key + \"Score\", key + \"_factor\"]);\r\n                        if (alt !== null) { flat[key] = alt; }\r\n                    }\r\n                });\r\n                return {\r\n                    nurse_id: c.nurse_id || c.staff_id || c.id,\r\n                    name: c.name || c.nurse_name || c.staff_name || c.real_name || (\"护理员 \" + (c.nurse_id || c.staff_id || \"\")),\r\n                    total: pickNumber(c, [\"total_score\", \"totalScore\", \"score\", \"total\", \"weighted_score\", \"final_score\"]),\r\n                    distance_km: pickNumber(c, [\"distance_km\", \"distanceKm\", \"distance\"]),\r\n                    order_count: pickNumber(c, [\"today_count\", \"order_count\", \"today_orders\", \"daily_orders\", \"orderCount\"]),\r\n                    rating: pickNumber(c, [\"rating\", \"rating_cached\", \"avg_score\"]),\r\n                    reason: c.reason || c.explain || \"\",\r\n                    factors: flat,\r\n                    raw: c\r\n                };\r\n            });\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        function candidateHtml(c, index, appointmentId) {\r\n            var badge = index === 0 ? '<span class=\"badge badge-red\">推荐</span>' : '<span class=\"badge badge-gray\">候选 ' + (index + 1) + '</span>';\r\n            var total = c.total === null ? \"-\" : (c.total <= 1 ? (c.total * 100).toFixed(1) : c.total.toFixed(1));\r\n            var bars = FACTOR_KEYS.map(function (key, i) {\r\n                var value = c.factors[key];\r\n                var width = pctOf(value);\r\n                var text = value === null ? \"无数据\" : (value <= 1 ? (value * 100).toFixed(0) + \"%\" : value.toFixed(1));\r\n                var weight = c.raw.weights ? c.raw.weights[i] : WEIGHTS[i];\r\n                var label = FACTOR_LABELS[i] + \"（权重 \" + (weight * 100).toFixed(0) + \"%）\";\r\n                var contribution = c.raw.contributions ? c.raw.contributions[key] : null;\r\n                return '<div class=\"factor\">' +\r\n                    '<div class=\"factor-head\"><span>' + label + '</span><span>' + text + '</span></div>' +\r\n                    '<div class=\"progress ' + (i === 0 ? \"gold\" : \"\") + '\"><span style=\"width:' + width.toFixed(1) + '%\"></span></div>' +\r\n                    (contribution !== null ? '<div class=\"small muted\">加权贡献 ' + (Number(contribution) * 100).toFixed(2) + ' 分</div>' : '') +\r\n                    \"</div>\";\r\n            }).join(\"\");\r\n            var meta = [];\r\n            if (c.distance_km !== null) { meta.push(\"距离 \" + c.distance_km.toFixed(2) + \" km\"); }\r\n            if (c.order_count !== null) { meta.push(\"当日 \" + c.order_count + \" 单\"); }\r\n            if (c.rating !== null) { meta.push(\"评分 \" + c.rating.toFixed(1)); }\r\n            return '<div class=\"card\" style=\"margin-bottom:14px\"><div class=\"card-body\">' +\r\n                '<div class=\"flex-between mb-1\"><div style=\"font-size:20px;font-weight:800\">' + badge + \" \" + App.esc(c.name) + \"</div>\" +\r\n                '<div class=\"badge badge-blue\" style=\"font-size:17px\">综合分 ' + total + \"</div></div>\" +\r\n                (meta.length ? '<div class=\"small muted mb-1\">' + App.esc(meta.join(\" · \")) + \"</div>\" : \"\") +\r\n                (c.reason ? '<div class=\"demo-tip\">评分依据：' + App.esc(c.reason) + \"</div>\" : \"\") +\r\n                bars +\r\n                (c.raw.penalty < 1 ? '<div class=\"notice warn\">重点关注降权：总分 × ' + App.esc(c.raw.penalty) + '</div>' : '') +\r\n                '<div class=\"btn-row mt-1\"><button class=\"btn btn-success btn-sm\" data-assign=\"' + appointmentId +\r\n                '\" data-nurse=\"' + (c.nurse_id || \"\") + '\">✓ 一键派单给 TA</button></div>' +\r\n                \"</div></div>\";\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        function openCandidates(appointmentId, code, itemName) {\r\n            var modal = App.modal({\r\n                title: \"派单候选 · \" + (code || appointmentId),\r\n                html: '<div id=\"cand-box\"><div class=\"empty-state\">正在计算候选护理员…</div></div>' +\r\n                    '<div class=\"demo-tip\">评分规则：四项因子分别乘实际权重后求和；重点关注护理员再降权，取 Top {{ top_n }}。权重以候选明细为准。</div>',\r\n                buttons: [{ text: \"关闭\", cls: \"btn-secondary\" }]\r\n            });\r\n            var box = modal.el.querySelector(\"#cand-box\");\r\n            App.api.get(\"/dispatch/\" + appointmentId + \"/candidates\").then(function (res) {\r\n                var list = normalize(res);\r\n                if (!list.length) {\r\n                    box.innerHTML = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><span class=\"icon\">🙁</span>暂无符合条件的候选护理员（可能技能/距离/当日单量受限）。人工指派仍需满足全部约束，可先核对资源或调整预约时间。</div>' +\r\n                        manualHtml(appointmentId);\r\n                    bindManual(modal.el, appointmentId);\r\n                    return;\r\n                }\r\n                box.innerHTML = list.map(function (c, i) { return candidateHtml(c, i, appointmentId); }).join(\"\") +\r\n                    '<div class=\"demo-tip\">若候选均不合适，可人工指派：</div>' + manualHtml(appointmentId);\r\n                bindManual(modal.el, appointmentId);\r\n            }).catch(function (err) {\r\n                box.innerHTML = '<div class=\"notice warn\">候选接口暂不可用：' + App.esc(err && err.message ? err.message : \"未知错误\") +\r\n                    '。接口就绪后此处将展示 Top{{ top_n }} 与四项因子明细；当前可人工指派：</div>' +\r\n                    '<div class=\"notice\">服务项目：<strong>' + App.esc(itemName || \"-\") + \"</strong></div>\" + manualHtml(appointmentId);\r\n                bindManual(modal.el, appointmentId);\r\n            });\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        function manualHtml(appointmentId) {\r\n            var options = NURSES.map(function (n) {\r\n                return '<option value=\"' + n.id + '\">' + App.esc(n.name) + \"（\" + App.esc(n.employee_no) +\r\n                    \" · 评分 \" + Number(n.rating || 0).toFixed(1) + \"）</option>\";\r\n            }).join(\"\");\r\n            return '<div class=\"form-field\"><label>人工指派护理员</label><select id=\"manual-nurse-' + appointmentId + '\">' +\r\n                '<option value=\"\">请选择护理员</option>' + options + \"</select></div>\" +\r\n                '<div class=\"btn-row mt-1\"><button class=\"btn btn-primary btn-sm\" data-assign=\"' + appointmentId + '\" data-nurse=\"\">✓ 确认指派</button></div>';\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        function bindManual(root, appointmentId) {\r\n            root.addEventListener(\"click\", function (ev) {\r\n                var btn = ev.target.closest(\"[data-assign]\");\r\n                if (!btn) { return; }\r\n                var nurseId = btn.getAttribute(\"data-nurse\");\r\n                if (!nurseId) {\r\n                    var sel = root.querySelector(\"#manual-nurse-\" + appointmentId);\r\n                    nurseId = sel ? sel.value : \"\";\r\n                }\r\n                if (!nurseId) { App.toast(\"请选择护理员\", \"warn\"); return; }\r\n                App.action(btn, \"/dispatch/\" + appointmentId + \"/assign\",\r\n                    { nurse_id: Number(nurseId) }, \"派单成功，已生成工单\");\r\n            });\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (ev) {\r\n            var btn = ev.target.closest(\"[data-dispatch]\");\r\n            if (!btn) { return; }\r\n            openCandidates(btn.getAttribute(\"data-dispatch\"), btn.getAttribute(\"data-code\"), btn.getAttribute(\"data-item\"));\r\n        });\r\n    })();\r\n</script>\r\n{% endblock %}\r\n"}}

Add File: D:\\Program Files (x86)\\巅峰\\python\\eldercare-platform\\docs\\chuanzhi\\智能派单完善.md\n+# 智能派单：候选排序、权重解释与无人可派处理\n+\n+日期：2026年11月12日。用户在预约反馈完善后提出：“在智能派单完善候选排序、权重解释、无人可派时的处理”。本次记录真实后续开发与验收，不替代完整Prompt聊天导出。\n+\n+## 操作方式\n+\n+1. 网格员在预约页面受理预约，进入“智能派单”查看已受理或待派单预约。\n+2. 点击“查看候选”，查看本次实际生效的权重、筛选统计、排名和每项加权贡献。\n+3. 选择合格候选派单。服务器重新检查技能、服务半径、当日负荷与时间冲突；成功后显示工单号及查看工单入口。\n+4. 无人可派时，按筛除原因核对人员技能、位置、服务半径及排班，或与老人协商新的预约时间。点击重新计算可复查最新资源。\n+5. 仍需协调时填写原因，申请人工协调。系统记录日志、审计并向本网格员及绑定家属发送站内通知；预约保持原状态，不会自动安排人员或绕过硬约束。\n+\n+## 候选排序与分数\n+\n+先执行硬条件筛选，再计算四因子评分。只有在岗、满足必需技能、位置与服务半径有效、服务当日未达工单上限且时段无在途工单冲突的护理员才能进入推荐。\n+\n+默认综合分为`100 × (技能×0.4 + 距离×0.3 + 负荷×0.2 + 评分×0.1)`。重点关注护理员在加权后再乘`0.8`。运行期`dispatch.weights`优先于环境默认；配置解析失败安全回退，页面说明实际来源。所有权重归一化为和为1。\n+\n+| 因子 | 含义与边界 |\n+|---|---|\n+| 技能 | 必需技能全部具备才可入围；合格者得1，因此该权重对合格者提供相同贡献 |\n+| 距离 | `max(0, 1 - 直线距离/有效半径)`；有效半径取系统与本人服务半径较小值；不代表实际交通耗时 |\n+| 负荷 | `max(0, 1 - 服务当日工单数/每日上限)`；不含取消工单，使用预约服务日而非查看页面当天 |\n+| 评分 | 近30天评价均分/5；没有可用评价时采用明确标示的默认评分，不使用资源池缓存冒充本次评分 |\n+\n+排序使用未舍入的最终总分，先按总分降序，再按实际距离升序、服务当日负荷升序、护理员ID升序。页面展示值可以四舍五入，显示同分时仍保留服务器排名，不在浏览器重新排序。默认返回配置的TopN；未展示的合格者不会被记为筛除。\n+\n+原实现先把因素和总分舍入至四位再排序，接近分数可能碰撞；本轮保留精确因子与贡献，分数展示与排名计算分开。非法或非有限定位不再被计算为有效距离。\n+\n+## 无人可派与接口失败\n+\n+报告展示检查总人数、合格人数及首个失败原因的互斥计数：已尝试、停用、缺少技能、定位/半径异常、超出半径、当日满负荷、时段冲突。一个人只计入首个不满足条件的原因，所以合格人数加筛除人数等于检查总人数；该计数不代表每人只存在一个问题。\n+\n+无人可派时显示原因和处理建议，提供重新计算、查看预约及人工协调申请。页面不再从全员资源池提供一个看似可绕过筛选的人工指派入口。候选接口断网、超时、损坏响应或权限失败时，明确说明推荐结果不可用，不冒称检查后无人可派，也不开放指派。\n+\n+协调申请在提交时重新检查：若资源已恢复而出现合格候选，则拒绝旧的无人申请并提示重新计算。重复申请同一预约状态阶段返回原记录，不重复发通知、不覆盖原原因。状态发生变化后重新进入待派单，可以形成新阶段的协调记录。\n+\n+派单与协调写操作等待时暂停按钮；成功结果持续显示。断网、超时、5xx或无法识别的成功响应不等于服务器未处理，页面提示先核对预约/工单结果并停止本页重复提交。推荐是只读检查，不占用护理员时段；最终派单仍重新验证。\n+\n+## 验证与材料边界\n+\n+本轮使用独立合成测试库与隔离浏览器，不修改实际运行数据库。测试结果和可复验报告在本轮验收完成后填入。\n+\n+SQLite下的应用级检查不能证明多个独立服务进程的数据库级并发排班互斥；本轮不将防双击或提交复检等同于该能力。人工协调为站内记录和通知，不承诺人员已安排或自动改期。历史PDF与演示视频未重新录制本次交互；以本轮源码、本文和证据为准。本次也是实际后续Prompt引导，不能据此虚构比赛所需的三条独立完整Prompt链。\n*** End Patch"));

````

tuple(get_dispatch_weight_details(db)[\"values\"])\r\n\r\n\r\ndef get_dispatch_weight_details(db: Session) -> dict[str, Any]:\r\n    \"\"\"Resolve the actual normalized weights without evaluating unsafe settings.weights_tuple.\"\"\"\r\n    row = db.execute(select(ConfigItem).where(ConfigItem.key == DISPATCH_WEIGHTS_KEY)).scalar_one_or_none()\r\n    fallback = False\r\n\r\n    def valid(raw: Any) -> bool:\r\n        # Two distinct fallbacks distinguish a valid result from parse failure.\r\n        return parse_weights(raw, (1, 0, 0, 0)) == parse_weights(raw, (0, 1, 0, 0))\r\n\r\n    if row is not None:\r\n        if valid(row.value):\r\n            return {\"values\": list(parse_weights(row.value)), \"source\": \"runtime\",\r\n                    \"source_label\": \"运行期配置\", \"fallback\": False}\r\n        fallback = True\r\n    if valid(settings.dispatch_weights):\r\n        return {\"values\": list(parse_weights(settings.dispatch_weights)), \"source\": \"environment\",\r\n                \"source_label\": \"环境配置\", \"fallback\": fallback}\r\n    return {\"values\": list(DEFAULT_WEIGHTS), \"source\": \"default\",\r\n            \"source_label\": \"内置默认权重\", \"fallback\": True}\r\n\r\n\r\n# ---------------- 工具 ----------------\r\ndef item_duration(item: ServiceItem | None) -> timedelta:\r\n    minutes = DEFAULT_ORDER_MINUTES\r\n    if item is not None and item.standard_duration_min:\r\n        minutes = max(1, int(item.standard_duration_min))\r\n    return timedelta(minutes=minutes)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _day_window(now: datetime) -> prefix = f\"WO{day:%Y%m%d}\"\r\n    codes = db.execute(select(WorkOrder.code).where(WorkOrder.code.like(f\"{prefix}%\"))).scalars().all()\r\n    seq = 0\r\n    for code in codes:\r\n        tail = code[len(prefix):]\r\n        if tail.isdigit():\r\n            seq = max(seq, int(tail))\r\n    return f\"{prefix}{seq + 1:04d}\"\r\n\r\n\r\ndef count_today_orders(db: Session, nurse_id: int, now: datetime | None = None) -> int:\r\n    \"\"\"护理员当日已派工单数（不含已取消）。\"\"\"\r\n    now = now or datetime.now()\r\n    start, end = _day_window(now)\r\n    total = db.scalar(\r\n        select(func.count())\r\n        .select_from(WorkOrder)\r\n        .where(\r\n            WorkOrder.nurse_id == nurse_id,\r\n            WorkOrder.status != OrderStatus.CANCELLED.value,\r\n            WorkOrder.scheduled_start >= start,\r\n            WorkOrder.scheduled_start < end,\r\n        )\r\n    )\r\n    return int(total or 0)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef find_busy_order(\r\n    db: Session,\r\n    nurse_id: int,\r\n    start: datetime,\r\n    end: datetime,\r\n    *,\r\n    exclude_nurse_ids: set[int] | None = None,\r\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\r\n    \"\"\"返回 TopN 候选护理员及四项因子明细（按 score 倒序）。\"\"\"\r\n    return get_candidate_report(db, appointment_id, user, limit=limit, now=now,\r\n                                exclude_nurse_ids=exclude_nurse_ids)[\"candidates\"]\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _current_coordination(db: Session, appointment_id: int) -> AppointmentLog | None:\r\n    boundary = db.scalar(select(func.max(AppointmentLog.id)).where(\r\n        AppointmentLog.appointment_id == appointment_id,\r\n        AppointmentLog.action.in_((\"dispatch\", \"requeue\", \"accept\")),\r\n    )) or 0\r\n    return db.scalars(select(AppointmentLog).where(\r\n        AppointmentLog.appointment_id == appointment_id,\r\n        AppointmentLog.action == \"dispatch_coordinate\", AppointmentLog.id > boundary,\r\n    ).order_by(AppointmentLog.id.desc())).first()\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _coordination_to_dict(record: AppointmentLog | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\r\n    if record is None:\r\n        return None\r\n    return {\"id\": record.id, \"reason\": record.remark, \"requested_at\": record.created_at}\r\n\r\n\r\ndef get_candidate_report(\r\n    db: Session, appointment_id: int, user: User | None, *, limit: int | None = None,\r\n    now: datetime | None = None, exclude_nurse_ids: set[int] | None = None,\r\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\r\n    \"\"\"只读推荐报告；首个硬约束失败互斥计数，与评分算法共用筛选口径。\"\"\"\r\n    appointment = appt_service.get_or_404(db, appointment_id)\r\n    if user is not None:\r\n        appt_service.ensure_visible(db, user, appointment)\r\n    if str(appointment.status) in appt_service.TERMINAL_APPOINTMENT_STATUSES:\r\n        raise Conflict(\"预约单已结束，无法推荐候选护理员\")\r\n\r\n    elder = db.get(Elder, appointment.elder_id)\r\n    item = db.get(ServiceItem, appointment.service_item_id)\r\n    if elder is None or elder.is_deleted:\r\n        raise NotFound(\"老人档案不存在\")\r\n    if not is_item_available(item):\r\n        raise RuleViolation(\"服务项目已下架或不存在，不可派单\")\r\n    duration = item_duration(item)\r\n    start = appointment.desired_start\r\n    end = start + duration\r\n    now = now or datetime.now()\r\n\r\n    top_n = max(1, min(20, int(settings.dispatch_top_n if limit is None else limit)))\r\n    weight_info = get_dispatch_weight_details(db)\r\n    weights = weight_info[\"values\"]\r\n    daily_limit = int(settings.order_daily_limit)\r\n    radius = float(settings.service_radius_km)\r\n\r\n    staffs = db.execute(select(Staff).order_by(Staff.id.asc())).scalars().all()\r\n    # 近 30 Evaluation.created_at <= now)\r\n        .group_by(Evaluation.nurse_id)\r\n    ).all())\r\n    day_start, day_end = _day_window(start)\r\n    loads = dict(db.execute(\r\n        select(WorkOrder.nurse_id, func.count(WorkOrder.id)).where(\r\n            WorkOrder.scheduled_start >= day_start, WorkOrder.scheduled_start < day_end,\r\n            WorkOrder.status != OrderStatus.CANCELLED.value,\r\n        ).group_by(WorkOrder.nurse_id)\r\n    ).all())\r\n    raw_candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\r\n    for staff in staffs:\r\n        distance = haversine(elder.lat, elder.lng, staff.base_lat, staff.base_lng)\r\n        raw_candidates.append(\r\n            {\r\n                \"staff_id\": staff.id,\r\n                \"name\": staff.name,\r\n                \"distance_km\": None if distance == float(\"inf\") else distance,\r\n                \"today_count\": loads.get(staff.id, 0),\r\n                \"skills\": list(staff.skills or []),\r\n                \"rating\": ratings.get(staff.id),\r\n                \"is_focus\": bool(staff.is_focus),\r\n                \"service_radius_km\": float(staff.service_radius_km or 0),\r\n                \"status\": staff.status,\r\n                \"daily_limit\": daily_limit,\r\n            }\r\n        )\r\n\r\n    definitions = (\r\n        (\"previously_attempted\", \"本轮已尝试\", \"联系其他护理员或由网格员人工协调\"),\r\n        (\"inactive\", \"护理员已停用\", \"核对人员状态或安排在岗护理员\"),\r\n        (\"missing_skills\", \"缺少必需技能\", \"安排具备项目所需技能的护理员\"),\r\n        (\"invalid_location\", \"定位或服务半径无效\", \"补充有效定位并核对服务半径\"),\r\n        (\"out_of_radius\", \"超出服务半径\", \"协调可覆盖该地址的护理员\"),\r\n        (\"daily_limit\", \"服务当日负荷已满\", \"调整服务日期或协调其他护理员\"),\r\n        (\"time_conflict\", \"服务时段冲突\", \"调整服务时段或协调其他护理员\"),\r\n    )\r\n    filter_counts = {code: 0 for code, _, _ in definitions}\r\n    available = []\r\n    required = list(item.required_skills or required_skills=required, radius_km=radius, daily_limit=daily_limit,\r\n        )\r\n        if rejection is None and find_busy_order(db, int(row[\"staff_id\"]), start, end) is not None:\r\n            rejection = \"time_conflict\"\r\n        if rejection is None:\r\n            available.append(row)\r\n        else:\r\n            filter_counts[rejection] += 1\r\n    scored = score_candidates(\r\n        available,\r\n        weights,\r\n        radius_km=radius,\r\n        daily_limit=daily_limit,\r\n        rating_default=float(settings.nurse_rating_default),\r\n        required_skills=required,\r\n        top_n=None,\r\n    )\r\n    for row in scored:\r\n        row[\"appointment_id\"] = appointment.id\r\n        row[\"desired_start\"] = sum(filter_counts.values()),\r\n        \"filter_counts\": [{\"code\": code, \"label\": label, \"count\": filter_counts[code], \"suggestion\": suggestion}\r\n                          for code, label, suggestion in definitions],\r\n        \"weights\": weight_info,\r\n        \"rules\": {\"radius_km\": radius, \"daily_limit\": daily_limit,\r\n                  \"rating_default\":   \"skill_note\": \"必需技能为硬约束；合格候选技能因子均为1，技能权重不区分合格者\"},\r\n        \"coordination\": _coordination_to_dict(_current_coordination(db, appointment.id)),\r\n        \"outcome\": \"ready\" if scored else \"no_candidates\", \"top_n\": top_n,\r\n    }\r\n\r\n\r\ndef coordinate_dispatch(\r\n    db: Session, appointment_id: int, *, reason: str, user: User | None, request: Request | None = None,\r\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\r\n    \"\"\"无人可派时记录协调请求；保留原状态，单阶段重复请求不重复通知。\"\"\"\r\n    reason = str(reason or \"\").strip()\r\n    if not reason or len(reason) > 255:\r\n        raise RuleViolation(\"协调原因必填且不超过255字\")\r\n    with db.begin_nested():\r\n        appointment = appt_service.get_or_404(db, appointment_id)\r\n        if user is not None:\r\n from_status = appointment.status\r\n    to_status = APPOINTMENT.next(from_status, \"dispatch\")\r\n\r\n    price_version_id, unit_price = (\r\n        catalog_service.price_snapshot(db, item) if item is not None else (None, Decimal(\"0.00\"))\r\n    )\r\n    order = WorkOrder(\r\n        code=_next_order_code(db, datetime.now().date()),\r\n        appointment_id=appointment.id,\r\n        elder_id=appointment.elder_id,\r\n        service_item_id=appointment.service_item_id,\r\n        nurse_id=staff.id,\r\n        status=OrderStatus.PENDING_ACCEPT.value,\r\n        scheduled_start=start,\r\n        scheduled_end=end,\r\n        price_version_id=price_version_id,\r\n        unit_price=unit_price,\r\n        quantity=1,\r\n        amount=catalog_service.q2(unit_price * 1),\r\n        community_org_id=appointment.community_org_id,\r\n        created_by=getattr(user, \"id\", None),\r\n        summary=remark,\r\n    )\r\n    db.add(order)\r\n    db.flush()\r\n\r\n    appointment.status = to_status\r\n    appt_service.write_log(\r\n        db,\r\n        appointment,\r\n        from_status=from_status,\r\n        to_status=to_status,\r\n        action=\"dispatch\",\r\n        operator_id=getattr(user, \"id\", None),\r\n        remark=f\"派单给 {staff.name}（工单 {order.code}）\",\r\n    )\r\n    _notify_nurse(\r\n        db,\r\n        staff,\r\n        title=\"新工单待接单\",\r\n        content=f\"{start:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} 上门服务工单 {order.code}，请在 {settings.accept_timeout_minutes} 分钟内接单\",\r\n        related_id=order.id,\r\n    )\r\n    _notify_family_and_grid(\r\n        db, appointment, title=\"预约已派单\",\r\n        content=f\"预约 {appointment.code} 已安排 {staff.name}，上门时间 {start:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M}\",\r\n    )\r\n    data = order_to_dict(db, order)\r\n    write_audit(\r\n        db,\r\n        user=user,\r\n        action=AuditAction.DISPATCH.value,\r\n reassign_order(\r\n    db: Session,\r\n    order_id: int,\r\n    nurse_id: int,\r\n    *,\r\n    reason: str,\r\n    user: User | None,\r\n    request: Request | None = None,\r\n) -> dict[str, }\r\n    remove() {}\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction harness(fetchImpl, firstTimer = 1) {\r\n    const body = new Element(\"body\");\r\n    const timers = new Map();\r\n    const cleared = [];\r\n    let nextTimer = firstTimer;\r\n    const window = {\r\n        location: { pathname: \"/appointments/new\", search: \"?mode=weekly\", href: \"\" },\r\n        setTimeout(callback, delay) {\r\n            const id = nextTimer++;\r\n            timers.set(id, { callback, delay });\r\n            return id;\r\n        },\r\n        clearTimeout(id) { cleared.push(id); timers.delete(id); },\r\n        addEventListener() {},\r\n    };\r\n    const storage = new Map();\r\n    const context = vm.createContext({\r\n        window,\r\n        document: {\r\n            body,\r\n            documentElement: { dataset: {} },\r\n            getElementById(id) { return body.children.find((child) => child.id === id) || null; }, setItem() {} },\r\n        sessionStorage: {\r\n            getItem(key) { return storage.get(key) || null; },\r\n            setItem(key, value) { storage.set(key, value); },\r\n            removeItem(key) { storage.delete(key); },\r\n        },\r\n    });\r\n    for (const script of scripts) vm.runInContext(script.source, context, { filename: script.name });\r\n    return {\r\n        App: window.App,\r\n        F: window.App.appointmentFeedback,\r\n        window, timers, cleared, body,\r\n        fire(delay) {\r\n            const entry = [...timers].find(([, timer]) => timer.delay === delay);\r\n            assert.ok(entry, `Expected a pending ${delay} ms timer`);\r\n            timers.delete(entry[0]);\r\n            entry[1].callback();\r\n        },\r\n    };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction response(status, payload, raw = false) {\r\n    return { status, ok: status >= 200 && status < 300, text: async () => raw ? payload : JSON.stringify(payload) };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction deferredFetch(capture) {\r\n    return (_url, init) => {\r\n        capture.signal = init.signal;\r\n        return new Promise((_resolve, reject) => {\r\n            init.signal.addEventListener(\"abort\", () => reject(new DOMException(\"Aborted\", \"AbortError\")), { once: true });\r\n        });\r\n    };\r\n}\r\n\r\ntest(\"opt-in request timeout aborts transport and produces REQUEST_TIMEOUT\", async () => {\r\n    const capture = {};\r\n    const h = harness(deferredFetch(capture));\r\n    const request = h.App.api.post(\"/appointments\", {}, { timeoutMs: 25 });\r\n    const rejected = assert.rejects(request, (error) => error.code === \"REQUEST_TIMEOUT\");\r\n    assert.equal(capture.signal.aborted, false);\r\n    h.fire(25);\r\n    await rejected;\r\n    assert.equal(capture.signal.aborted, true);\r\n"}}

var info = F.error(err, reading ? \"preview\" : \"dispatch\");\r\n        if (reading) {\r\n            if (!info.login) { info.title = \"候选计算暂未完成\"; }\r\n            info.next = }).join(\"\");\r\n                if (!d.coordination) {\r\n                    coordination.innerHTML = '<div class=\"form-field\"><label for=\"coordination-reason\">申请人工协调原因（必填，最多500字）</label>' +\r\n                        '<textarea id=\"coordination-reason\" maxlength=\"500\" aria-describedby=\"dispatch-status\" placeholder=\"请说明已核对的资源情况和需要协调的事项\"></textarea></div>' +\r\n                        '<button type=\"button\" class=\"btn btn-primary mt-1\" data-coordinate>申请人工协调</button>' +\r\n                        '<p class=\"hint\">预约仍保留当前状态；协调成功记录不表示已生成工单。</p>';\r\n                }\r\n            } else {\r\n                F.show(status, { title: \"候选计算完成\", detail: \"以下护理员均通过本次硬约束检查。可按排名选择，提交派单时仍会重新校验。\", tone: \"success\" });\r\n            }\r\n            if (d.coordination) {\r\n                var note = document.createElement(\"div\");\r\n                F.show(note, { title: \"已申请人工协调 · 记录 \" + d.coordination.id, detail: \"原因：\" + d.coordination.reason, next: \"申请时间：\" + d.coordination.requested_at + \"。当前阶段不重复提交；尚未因此创建工单。\", tone: \"success\" });\r\n                coordination.appendChild(note);\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        function calculate() {\r\n            if (pending || state.locked) { return; });\r\n            F.pending(status, operation === \"assign\" ? \"正在派单并再次校验硬约束…\" : \"正在记录人工协调申请…\");\r\n            App.api.post(\"/dispatch/\" + appointmentId + \"/\" + operation, body, { timeoutMs: D.timeoutMs }).then(function (res) {\r\n                var d = res && res.data;\r\n                if (!res || res.code !== 0 || !d || (operation === \"assign\" ?\r\n                    !id(d.id) || typeof d.code !== \"string\" || !d.code || d.appointment_id !== appointmentId || d.nurse_id !== nurseId || d.status !== \"pending_accept\" :\r\n                    d.appointment_id !== appointmentId || !validCoordination(d.coordination) || typeof d.already_recorded !== \"boolean\" ||\r\n                    d.work_order_created !== false || !count(d.notification_count))) { throw invalid(); }\r\n                pending = false;\r\n                root.removeAttribute(\"aria-busy\");\r\n                root.querySelectorAll(\".modal-close, .modal-foot button\").forEach(function (el) { el.disabled = false; });\r\n                if (operation === \"assign\") {\r\n                    state.locked = true;\r\n                    var message = \"派单成功，已生成工单 \" + d.code + \"。\";\r\n                    F.success(status, message, \"本次操作已确认，工单当前待护理员接单。请打开工单详情查看；本页不会自动刷新或重复派单。\");\r\n                    F.success(feedback, message, \"当前预约表格尚未刷新，以上派单结果已确认。\");\r\n                    link(status, \"打开工单详情\", \"/orders/\" + d.id);\r\n                    link(feedback, \"打开工单详情\", \"/orders/\" + d.id);\r\n                    lockRow(button, \"已派单，工单号 \" + d.code + \"。请通过上方工单详情查看。\");\r\n                } else {\r\n                    var updated = Object.assign({}, latest, { coordination: d.coordination });\r\n                    render(updated);\r\n                    calc.disabled = false;\r\n                    button.disabled = false;\r\n                    F.success(feedback, (d.already_recorded ? \"本阶段此前已申请人工协调\" : \"人工协调申请已记录\") + \"，记录 \" + d.coordination.id + \"。\", \"原因：\" + d.coordination.reason + \"。预约状态保持不变，没有创建工单；后续由工作人员核对资源与预约时间。\");\r\n                }\r\n            }).catch(function (err) {\r\n                pending = false;\r\n                root.removeAttribute(\"aria-busy\");\r\n                var info = failure(status, err, operation);\r\n                failure(feedback, err, operation);\r\n                root.querySelectorAll(\".modal-close, .modal-foot button\").forEach(function (el) { el.disabled = false; });\r\n                if (reasonInput) { reasonInput.disabled = false; }\r\n                if (info.uncertain || info.login) {\r\n                    state.locked = true;\r\n                    lockRow(button, info.login ? \"请重新登录后核对派单结果。\" : \"写入结果尚未确认，请先查看预约及工单，勿重复操作。\");\r\n                } else {\r\n                    latest = null;\r\n                    calc.disabled = false;\r\n                    button.disabled = false;\r\n                    var note = document.createElement(\"p\");\r\n                    note.textContent = \"此前候选已失效，请重新计算后再提交。当前填写原因仍保留。\";\r\n                    status.appendChild(note);\r\n                }\r\n            });\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        root.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\r\n            var target = event.target.closest(\"[data-recalculate], [data-assign-nurse], [data-coordinate]\");\r\n            if (!target || target.disabled) { return; }\r\n            if (target.hasAttribute(\"data-recalculate\")) { calculate(); }\r\n            else if (target.hasAttribute(\"data-coordinate\")) { write(\"coordinate\"); }\r\n            else { write(\"assign\", Number(target.getAttribute(\"data-assign-nurse\"))); }\r\n        });\r\n        root.addEventListener(\"keydown\", function (event) {\r\n            if (pending && event.key === \"Escape\") { event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation(); }\r\n        }, true);\r\n        root.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\r\n            if (pending && event.target === root) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation(); }\r\n        }, true);\r\n        calculate();\r\n    };\r\n    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\r\n        var button = event.target.closest(\"[data-dispatch]\");\r\n        if (button && !button.disabled) { D.open(button); phi1\r\n    d_lambda = math.radians(float(lng2) - float(lng1))\r\n    a = math.sin(d_phi / 2) ** 2 + math.cos(phi1) * math.cos(phi2) * math.sin(d_lambda / 2) ** 2\r\n    return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_KM * math.asin(math.sqrt(min(1.0, max(0.0, a))))\r\n\r\n\r\ndef skill_factor(required: Sequence[str] | None, staff_skills: Sequence[str] | None) -> float:\r\n    \"\"\"技能覆盖率：必需技能全部覆盖得 1.0，部分覆盖按比例，无要求得 1.0。\"\"\"\r\n    required_list = [str(s) for s in (required or []) if str(s).strip()]\r\n    if not required_list:\r\n        return 1.0\r\n    owned = {str(s) for s in (staff_skills or [])}\r\n    covered = sum(1 for skill in required_list if skill in owned)\r\n    return _clamp(covered / len(required_list))\r\n\r\n\r\ndef distance_factor(distance_km: float, radius_km: float = DEFAULT_RADIUS_KM) -> float:\r\n    \"\"\"距离得分：越近越高；超出服务半径得 0；半径非法时仅 0 距离得满分。\"\"\"\r\n    distance = _finite_number(distance_km)\r\n    radius = _finite_number(radius_km)\r\n    if distance is None or distance < 0 or radius is None:\r\n        return 0.0\r\n    if radius <= 0:\r\n        return 1.0 if distance <= 0 else 0.0\r\n    return _clamp(1.0 - max(0.0, distance) / radius)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef load_factor(today_count: int, limit: int = DEFAULT_DAILY_LIMIT) -> float:\r\n    \"\"\"当日负荷得分：已派 0 单得 1.0，达到上限得 0。\"\"\"\r\n    daily_limit = int(limit)\r\n    if daily_limit <= 0:\r\n        return 0.0\r\n    return _clamp(1.0 - max(0, int(today_count)) / daily_limit)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef rating_factor(rating: float | None, default: float = DEFAULT_RATING) -> float:\r\n    \"\"\"评分得分：评分/5，缺失或非法时用有效默认评分。\"\"\"\r\n    value = _finite_number(rating)\r\n    busy = find_busy_order(db, staff.id, start, end, exclude_order_id=exclude_order_id)\r\n    if busy is not None:\r\n        raise Conflict(\r\n            f\"护理员「{staff.name}」在 {busy.scheduled_start:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} 已有在途工单 {busy.code}，时段冲突\"\r\n        )\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _ensure_eligible(db: Session, staff: Staff, elder: Elder | None, item: ServiceItem | None) -> None:\r\n    \"\"\"手工派单与改派同样必须满足推荐算法的硬约束。\"\"\"\r\n    if not is_item_available(item):\r\n        raise RuleViolation(\"服务项目已下架或不存在，不可派单\")\r\n    if elder is None or elder.is_deleted:\r\n        raise NotFound(\"老人档案不存在\")\r\n    missing = set(item.required_skills or []) - set(staff.skills or [])\r\n    if missing:\r\n        raise RuleViolation(\"护理员缺少必需技能：\" + \"、\".join(sorted(missing)))\r\n    distance = haversine(elder.lat, elder.lng, staff.base_lat, staff.base_lng)\r\n    radii = (float(settings.service_radius_km), float(staff.service_radius_km or 0))\r\n    radius = min(radii)\r\n    if not math.isfinite(distance) or any(not math.isfinite(value) or value <= 0 for value in radii) or distance > radius:\r\n        raise RuleViolation(\"护理员超出服务半径或缺少定位坐标\")\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _notify_family_and_grid(db: Session, appointment: Appointment, *, title: str, content: str) -> int:\r\n    elder = db.get(Elder, appointment.elder_id)\r\n    if elder is None:\r\n        return 0\r\n    recipients = set(db.scalars(\r\n        select(User.id).join(Role, User.role_id == Role.id).where(\r\n            User.is_active.is_(True),\r\n            User.org_id == elder.grid_org_id,\r\n            Role.code == RoleCode.GRID.value,\r\n        )\r\n    ).all())\r\n    if elder.user_id:\r\n        recipients.add(elder.user_id)\r\n    for recipient in sorted(recipients):\r\n        db.add(Notification(\r\n            user_id=recipient, elder_id=elder.id,\r\n            channel=NotificationChannel.INAPP.value, title=title, content=content,\r\n"}

````text
已完成智能派单完善：

- **候选排序**：按精确总分排序，同分依次比较距离、当日负荷和编号。
- **权重解释**：展示实际生效权重、各项贡献、默认评分及重点关注降权。
- **无人可派**：显示筛除原因和建议，支持重新计算、申请人工协调；记录原因并通知，不重复发通知、不自动生成工单。
- **异常反馈**：区分接口故障与无人可派，结果不明时暂停重复提交。

验证通过：**508 项后端测试、87 项前端检查、31 项浏览器检查**，覆盖率 **89.35%**。

Session,\r\n    operator: User,\r\n    payload: Any,\r\n    request: Request | None = None,\r\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\r\n    \"\"\"更新/新增运行期配置（支持单项、批量 items、键值映射三种写法）。\"\"\"\r\n    pairs = _normalize_config_payload(payload)\r\n    changed: dict[str, Any] = {}\r\n    aliases = (\"dispatch.weights\", \"dispatch_weights\")\r\n    # Validate the entire batch before mutating even unrelated configuration.\r\n    for key, value in pairs:\r\n        if not key:\r\n            raise RuleViolation(\"配置 key 不能为空\")\r\n        if value is None:\r\n            raise RuleViolation(f\"配置 {key} 的值不能为空\")\r\n        if key in aliases:\r\n            value = _normalize_weights(value)\r\n        text = _serialize_config(value)\r\n        if len(text) > 512:\r\n            raise RuleViolation(f\"配置 {key} 的值过长（最多 512 字符）\")\r\n        changed[key] = value\r\n    if all(key in changed for key in aliases):\r\n        first, second = (parse_weights(changed[key]) for key in aliases)\r\n        if any(not math.isclose(left, right, rel_tol=0, abs_tol=1e-12) for left, right in zip(first, second, strict=True)):\r\n            raise RuleViolation(\"同批派单权重别名的值不一致，请使用相同权重\")\r\n        # Equivalent proportions should also be stored identically.\r\n        changed[aliases[1]] = changed[aliases[0]]\r\n\r\n    with db.begin_nested():\r\n        existing = {item.key: item for item in db.scalars(select(ConfigItem)).all()}\r\n        for key, value in changed.items():\r\n            text = _serialize_config(value)\r\n            item = existing.get(key)\r\n            if item is None:\r\n                item = ConfigItem(key=key, value=text, description=f\"运行期配置 {key}\", updated_by=operator.id)\r\n                db.add(item)\r\n                existing[key] = item\r\n            else:\r\n                item.value = text\r\n                item.updated_by = operator.id\r\n        synced = {}\r\n        for key in aliases:\r\n            other = aliases[1] if key == aliases[0] else aliases[0]\r\n            if key in changed and other not in changed and other in existing:\r\n                existing[other].value = _serialize_config(changed[key])\r\n                existing[other].updated_by = operator.id\r\n                synced[other] = existing[other].value\r\n        write_audit(\r\n            db,\r\n            user=operator,\r\n            action=AuditAction.UPDATE,\r\n            entity_type=\"config\",\r\n            after={\"changed\": {key: _serialize_config(value) for key, value in changed.items()}, \"synced\": synced},\r\n            detail=f\"更新配置：{', '.join(changed)}\",\r\n            request=request,\r\n        )\r\n    db.commit()\r\n    return {\r\n        \"changed\": sorted(changed),\r\n        \"values\": {\r\n            key: get_config(db, key, _config_default(key) or DEFAULT_CONFIGS.get(key))\r\n            for key in changed\r\n        },\r\n        \"items\": [config_to_dict(row) for row in db.execute(select(ConfigItem)).scalars().all()],\r\n    }\r\n\r\n\r\n# ==========================================================================\r\n# 备份与恢复\r\n# ==========================================================================\r\ndef _session_factory_for(db: Session) -> sessionmaker:\r\n    \"\"\"基于当前请求会话的绑定引擎，创建独立 Session（不依赖请求上下文）。\"\"\"\r\n    bind = db.get_bind()\r\n    engine = getattr(bind, \"engine\", bind)\r\n    return sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, expire_on_commit=False, future=True)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef backup_to_dict(record: BackupRecord) -> dict[str, Any]:\r\n    file_path = record.file_path or \"\"\r\n    return {\r\n        \"id\": record.id,\r\n        \"file_path\": file_path,\r\n        \"file_name\": Path(file_path).name if file_path else \"\",\r\n        \"file_size\": record.file_size,\r\n        \"backup_type\": record.backup_type,\r\n        \"status\": record.status,\r\n        \"tables_snapshot\": record.tables_snapshot,\r\n        \"tables_count\": len(record.tables_snapshot or {}),\r\n        \"note\": record.note,\r\n        \"created_by\": record.created_by,\r\n        \"created_at\": record.created_at,\r\n"}
{}

派单页面解释与实际计算一致性\n+\n+日期：2026年11月12日。用户在智能派单完善后追加“页面解释与实际计算一致”。本轮以真实计算输入、接口结果和页面展示逐项核对，修复可触发的口径差异。\n+\n+## 已修复的不一致\n+\n+| 问题 | 实际修复 |\n+|---|---|\n+| 预约须知固定写3公里、6单、Top3 | 使用与推荐接口相同的策略函数，展示实际环境半径、服务当日上限和限定1–20后的TopN |\n+| 种子/管理页使用`dispatch_weights`，算法只读取`dispatch.weights` | 兼容旧键；有效新键优先，再尝试旧键、环境与内置默认；返回实际来源键 |\n+| 两个权重键同时存在，修改旧键可能无效 | 保存任一键时同步已存在的另一键；同批冲突值拒绝，审计记录同步变更 |\n+| 系统配置接口返回环境权重，被误认为生效权重 | 返回算法相同解析器的归一化实际值；页面区分实际值、环境默认和存储项 |\n+| 默认评分配置超出范围或非法时，规则说明与算法采用值不同 | 通过同一评分函数安全解析与限制到0–5，元信息和候选使用同值 |\n+| 距离先舍入四位再显示两位，存在双重舍入 | 页面直接由原始距离显示两位，计算过程展示原始距离 |\n+| 贡献相加或同显示分可能让用户误解排名 | 主卡保留简表，另可展开原始因子、权重、贡献和最终公式；说明舍入与精确排序的区别 |\n+\n+## 如何核对\n+\n+点击智能派单的“查看候选”，每张候选卡可展开“原始数值与计算过程”。页面展示本次计算时间、预约服务日期和起止时段；当日负荷使用预约服务日，评价使用计算时刻之前最近30天。\n+\n+每项贡献为`因子 × 本次实际归一化权重`。最终综合分为`四项贡献原值之和 × 重点关注系数 × 100`。重点关注系数来自后端算法常量，其余护理员系数为1。技能必须全部满足才可入围，因此合格者技能因子均为1。\n+\n+原始展开区展示接口提供的浮点数值；简表保留有限小数位。原始浮点运算可能出现如`30.000000000000004`的尾差，不使用这些格式化后的数字重新计算或排序。页面也会校验原始输入与因子公式、因子与贡献、贡献和与总分、关注标记与系数、精确分数与排名；响应字段不一致时不开放本次候选派单。\n+\n+派单权重支持保存后重新计算生效。半径、每日上限、TopN和默认评分仍从环境读取，需要调整环境并重启；系统配置页明确说明：仅保存这些同名配置项不会使派单算法热更新。页面顶部是打开页面时的快照，后续每次推荐以该次响应中的权重与时间为准。\n+\n+## 验证\n+\n+验证结果将在最终全量检查后记录。浏览器验收使用独立合成数据库和真实API响应，保存两组权重下的`weights-A/B-report.json`与对应`dom.json`，逐项比较因子输入、乘法贡献、加权和、系数、最终分及排名；不注入伪造的成功推荐结果。\n+\n+重点场景包括：旧种子权重保存后生效、显式新键同步、重点关注降权、默认评分、真实近30天评价、未来服务日负荷与当天负荷区分、取消工单排除、同显示分保留精确排名，以及390px原始长数字换行。旧版本默认评分合同首次9项中7项失败、2项通过的日志保留，用于说明本次修复前的真实问题。\n+\n+本轮原始API/DOM证据使用合成演示人员，不含真实数据库。它验证本次计算快照的一致性，不证明并发排班互斥、外部交通耗时或所有配置项均可热更新。PDF与视频仍为此前快照；本轮代码、文档与验收产物反映最新行为。本文记录真实用户后续需求，不替代完整原始对话。\n*** 派单页面解释与实际计算一致性\r\n\r\n日期：2026年11月12日。用户在智能派单完善后追加“页面解释与实际计算一致”。本轮以真实计算输入、接口结果和页面展示逐项核对，修复可触发的口径差异。\r\n\r\n## 已修复的不一致\r\n\r\n| 问题 | 实际修复 |\r\n|---|---|\r\n| 预约须知固定写3公里、6单、Top3 | 使用与推荐接口相同的策略函数，展示实际环境半径、服务当日上限和限定1–20后的TopN |\r\n| 种子/管理页使用`dispatch_weights`，算法只读取`dispatch.weights` | 兼容旧键；有效新键优先，再尝试旧键、环境与内置默认；返回实际来源键 |\r\n| 两个权重键同时存在，修改旧键可能无效 | 保存任一键时同步已存在的另一键；同批冲突值拒绝，审计记录同步变更 |\r\n| 系统配置接口返回环境权重，被误认为生效权重 | 返回算法相同解析器的归一化实际值；页面区分实际值、环境默认和存储项 |\r\n| 默认评分配置超出范围或非法时，规则说明与算法采用值不同 | 通过同一评分函数安全解析与限制到0–5，元信息和候选使用同值 |\r\n| 距离先舍入四位再显示两位，存在双重舍入 | 页面直接由原始距离显示两位，计算过程展示原始距离 |\r\n| 贡献相加或同显示分可能让用户误解排名 | 主卡保留简表，另可展开原始因子、权重、贡献和最终公式；说明舍入与精确排序的区别 |\r\n\r\n## 如何核对\r\n\r\n点击智能派单的“查看候选”，每张候选卡可展开“原始数值与计算过程”。页面展示本次计算时间、预约服务日期和起止时段；当日负荷使用预约服务日，评价使用计算时刻之前最近30天。\r\n\r\n每项贡献为`因子 × 本次实际归一化权重`。最终综合分为`四项贡献原值之和 × 重点关注系数 × 100`。重点关注系数来自后端算法常量，其余护理员系数为1。技能必须全部满足才可入围，因此合格者技能因子均为1。\r\n\r\n原始展开区展示接口提供的浮点数值；简表保留有限小数位。原始浮点运算可能出现如`30.000000000000004`的尾差，不使用这些格式化后的数字重新计算或排序。页面也会校验原始输入与因子公式、因子与贡献、贡献和与总分、关注标记与系数、精确分数与排名；响应字段不一致时不开放本次候选派单。\r\n\r\n派单权重支持保存后重新计算生效。半径、每日上限、TopN和默认评分仍从环境读取，需要调整环境并重启；系统配置页明确说明：仅保存这些同名配置项不会使派单算法热更新。页面顶部是打开页面时的快照，后续每次推荐以该次响应中的权重与时间为准。\r\n\r\n## 验证\r\n\r\n验证结果将在最终全量检查后记录。浏览器验收使用独立合成数据库和真实API响应，保存两组权重下的`weights-A/B-report.json`与对应`dom.json`，逐项比较因子输入、乘法贡献、加权和、系数、最终分及排名；不注入伪造的成功推荐结果。\r\n\r\n重点场景包括：旧种子权重保存后生效、显式新键同步、重点关注降权、默认评分、真实近30天评价、未来服务日负荷与当天负荷区分、取消工单排除、同显示分保留精确排名，以及390px原始长数字换行。旧版本默认评分合同首次9项中7项失败、2项通过的日志保留，用于说明本次修复前的真实问题。\r\n\r\n本轮原始API/DOM证据使用合成演示人员，不含真实数据库。它验证本次计算快照的一致性，不证明并发排班互斥、外部交通耗时或所有配置项均可热更新。PDF与视频仍为此前快照；本轮代码、文档与验收产物反映最新行为。本文记录真实用户后续需求，不替代完整原始对话。\r\n# 社区智慧养老服务与健康监测平台\r\n\r\n2026-11-12 智能派单完善：精确分数稳定排序，展示实际权重、因子贡献与降权；无人可派展示筛除原因，支持记录人工协调并防重复通知。最新全量 **508项测试通过，覆盖率89.35%**，前端87项、Edge31项检查通过。见[智能派单完善](docs/chuanzhi/智能派单完善.md)。\r\n\r\n2026-11-12 预约反馈完善：成功结果常驻并提供手动查看列表入口；业务拒绝说明原因并允许修改，断网、超时或异常响应提示先核对结果，防止重复提交。登录过期支持本账号临时草稿。实现与验证见[预约反馈完善](docs/chuanzhi/预约反馈完善.md)。\r\n\r\n2026-11-12 上一轮预约快照：单次/四周时段预检、长服务冲突检查、取消整体回滚与释放、失败保留输入。该快照 **438项测试通过，覆盖率89.17%**；该轮21项Edge检查通过。操作步骤和真实前后对照见[预约功能迭代](docs/chuanzhi/预约功能迭代.md)。\r\n\r\n> 传智杯本科B组｜2132417｜唐克波。公开仓库：https://github.com/KEBO-666/eldercare-platform 。已通过匿名克隆与全新安装启动验证，详见[GitHub发布记录](docs/chuanzhi/GitHub发布记录.md)。\r\n\r\n> 面向社区居家养老场景的一体化平台：老人档案（敏感字段加密 + 二次授权）、能力评估、服务目录与价格版本、\r\n> 预约三重校验、智能派单（四因子加权 Top3）、上门服务全流程留痕、健康数据接入与三级预警、\r\n> 评价投诉与回访、费用结算与政府补贴、统计看板、系统管理与审计（哈希链）。\r\n\r\n- **后端**：FastAPI + SQLAlchemy 2.0 + Pydantic v2 + SQLite / PostgreSQL\r\n- **前端**：Jinja2 服务端渲染 + 页面内 `fetch` 调用 REST API + Bootstrap 5 + ECharts 5（**全部本地静态资源，断网可演示**）\r\n- **安全**：JWT + RBAC（35 个权限点 / 6 类角色）+ 四级数据范围 + AES-256-GCM 字段加密 + HMAC 摘要检索 + 审计哈希链\r\n- **适老化**：正文 18px+、按钮 ≥44px、高对比配色、表格斑马纹、红色预警置顶、键盘可访问\r\n\r\n2026-11-12 已补齐四周周期预约、按派单时刻计算的超时顺延、取消/爽约释放时段、跨网格读写校验、账单明细范围控制与累计退费上限，并增加三级字号切换。改动、验证方法和实际能力边界见 [赛题验收优化说明](docs/赛题验收优化说明.md)。\r\n\r\n一键验收：`powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts/check.ps1`（Ruff → 全量测试与覆盖率 → 临时库种子幂等 → 真实 HTTP 六角色页面检查）。可穿戴模拟：`python -m scripts.simulate_wearable --elder-id 1 --scenario abnormal --dry-run`；移除 `--dry-run` 后交互登录并上报。\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 一、快速开始（Windows · 演示环境）\r\n\r\n前提：Windows PowerShell 5.1+、可用的Python
